{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"call_6567c42f885c4ca8bdb662f7","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t//! Critical path computation for dependency DAGs.\n2\t//!\n3\t//! This module implements a two-pass algorithm to compute float (slack) for each bead:\n4\t//! - Forward pass: compute earliest start (ES) - number of hops from root\n5\t//! - Backward pass: compute latest start (LS)\n6\t//! - Float = LS - ES (zero float = critical path)\n7\t//!\n8\t//! The critical path cache is used by claim scoring to prioritize beads that block\n9\t//! the longest chain of work.\n10\t\n11\tuse anyhow::Result;\n12\tuse rusqlite::{params, Connection};\n13\tuse serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};\n14\t\n15\t/// Result of critical path computation for an epic.\n16\t#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]\n17\tpub struct CriticalPathResult {\n18\t    /// The epic (root) bead ID\n19\t    pub epic_id: String,\n20\t    /// All beads in the epic with their float values\n21\t    pub beads: Vec<BeadFloat>,\n22\t    /// Longest chain of bead IDs from root to leaf\n23\t    pub longest_chain: Vec<String>,\n24\t    /// Minimum number of bead completions on critical path\n25\t    pub min_remaining: i64,\n26\t}\n27\t\n28\t/// Float information for a single bead.\n29\t#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]\n30\tpub struct BeadFloat {\n31\t    pub bead_id: String,\n32\t    pub epic_id: Option<String>,\n33\t    pub es: i64,    // earliest start\n34\t    pub ls: i64,    // latest start\n35\t    pub float: i64, // ls - es\n36\t}\n37\t\n38\t/// Invalidate the critical path cache.\n39\t///\n40\t/// Call this when the dependency graph changes (add/remove dependencies,\n41\t/// status changes that affect the graph).\n42\tpub fn invalidate_cache(tx: &Connection) -> Result<()> {\n43\t    tx.execute(\"DELETE FROM critical_path_cache\", [])?;\n44\t    Ok(())\n45\t}\n46\t\n47\t/// Compute critical path for all beads in the dependency graph.\n48\t///\n49\t/// This performs a two-pass walk:\n50\t/// 1. Forward pass: compute ES (earliest start) from roots to leaves\n51\t/// 2. Backward pass: compute LS (latest start) from leaves to roots\n52\t/// 3. Float = LS - ES (zero float beads are on critical path)\n53\t///\n54\t/// The results are cached in the critical_path_cache table for use by\n55\t/// claim scoring.\n56\t///\n57\t/// # Arguments\n58\t/// * `tx` - The transaction to use\n59\t///\n60\t/// # Returns\n61\t/// * `Ok(CriticalPathResult)` - Aggregated results with beads, longest chain, and min remaining\n62\tpub fn compute_all_critical_paths(tx: &Connection) -> Result<CriticalPathResult> {\n63\t    // Create temporary tables for the computation\n64\t    tx.execute(\n65\t        \"CREATE TEMP TABLE IF NOT EXISTS forward_pass (bead_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY, es INTEGER)\",\n66\t        [],\n67\t    )?;\n68\t    tx.execute(\"DELETE FROM forward_pass\", [])?;\n69\t\n70\t    tx.execute(\n71\t        \"CREATE TEMP TABLE IF NOT EXISTS backward_pass (bead_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY, ls INTEGER)\",\n72\t        [],\n73\t    )?;\n74\t    tx.execute(\"DELETE FROM backward_pass\", [])?;\n75\t\n76\t    // Forward pass: iterative computation of ES\n77\t    // Initialize roots (beads with no outgoing blocking dependencies) with ES = 0\n78\t    tx.execute(\n79\t        r#\"\n80\t        INSERT INTO forward_pass (bead_id, es)\n81\t        SELECT DISTINCT i.id, 0\n82\t        FROM issues i\n83\t        WHERE i.deleted_at IS NULL\n84\t          AND NOT EXISTS (\n85\t              SELECT 1 FROM dependencies d\n86\t              WHERE d.issue_id = i.id\n87\t                AND d.type IN ('blocks', 'parent-child', 'conditional-blocks', 'waits-for')\n88\t          )\n89\t        \"#,\n90\t        [],\n91\t    )?;\n92\t\n93\t    // Iteratively compute ES for dependent beads\n94\t    // We use a worklist approach: keep going until no more beads can be computed\n95\t    let mut max_iterations = 1000; // Safety limit\n96\t    let mut iteration = 0;\n97\t    loop {\n98\t        iteration += 1;\n99\t        if iteration > max_iterations {\n100\t            return Err(anyhow::anyhow!(\n101\t                \"Forward pass: too many iterations, possible cycle\"\n102\t            ));\n103\t        }\n104\t\n105\t        // Find beads whose all dependencies are already computed\n106\t        let affected = tx.execute(\n107\t            r#\"\n108\t            INSERT OR IGNORE INTO forward_pass (bead_id, es)\n109\t            SELECT DISTINCT d.issue_id, MAX(f.es) + 1\n110\t            FROM dependencies d\n111\t            INNER JOIN forward_pass f ON f.bead_id = d.depends_on_id\n112\t            WHERE d.type IN ('blocks', 'parent-child', 'conditional-blocks', 'waits-for')\n113\t              AND NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM forward_pass fp WHERE fp.bead_id = d.issue_id)\n114\t              AND NOT EXISTS (\n115\t                  SELECT 1 FROM dependencies d2\n116\t                  WHERE d2.issue_id = d.issue_id\n117\t                    AND d2.type IN ('blocks', 'parent-child', 'conditional-blocks', 'waits-for')\n118\t                    AND NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM forward_pass fp WHERE fp.bead_id = d2.depends_on_id)\n119\t              )\n120\t            GROUP BY d.issue_id\n121\t            \"#,\n122\t            [],\n123\t        )?;\n124\t        if affected == 0 {\n125\t            break;\n126\t        }\n127\t    }\n128\t\n129\t    // Backward pass: compute LS (latest start)\n130\t    // Initialize leaves (beads with no open dependents) with LS = ES\n131\t    tx.execute(\n132\t        r#\"\n133\t        INSERT INTO backward_pass (bead_id, ls)\n134\t        SELECT DISTINCT f.bead_id, f.es\n135\t        FROM forward_pass f\n136\t        WHERE NOT EXISTS (\n137\t            SELECT 1 FROM dependencies d\n138\t            INNER JOIN issues i ON i.id = d.issue_id\n139\t            WHERE d.depends_on_id = f.bead_id\n140\t              AND d.type IN ('blocks', 'parent-child', 'conditional-blocks', 'waits-for')\n141\t              AND i.status NOT IN ('closed', 'tombstone')\n142\t        )\n143\t        \"#,\n144\t        [],\n145\t    )?;\n146\t\n147\t    // Iteratively compute LS for beads whose all dependents are computed\n148\t    // Using MAX instead of MIN: parallel tasks get positive float\n149\t    iteration = 0;\n150\t    loop {\n151\t        iteration += 1;\n152\t        if iteration > max_iterations {\n153\t            return Err(anyhow::anyhow!(\n154\t                \"Backward pass: too many iterations, possible cycle\"\n155\t            ));\n156\t        }\n157\t\n158\t        let affected = tx.execute(\n159\t            r#\"\n160\t            INSERT OR IGNORE INTO backward_pass (bead_id, ls)\n161\t            SELECT DISTINCT f.bead_id, MAX(b.ls) - 1\n162\t            FROM forward_pass f\n163\t            INNER JOIN dependencies d ON d.depends_on_id = f.bead_id\n164\t            INNER JOIN backward_pass b ON b.bead_id = d.issue_id\n165\t            WHERE d.type IN ('blocks', 'parent-child', 'conditional-blocks', 'waits-for')\n166\t              AND NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM backward_pass bp WHERE bp.bead_id = f.bead_id)\n167\t              AND NOT EXISTS (\n168\t                  SELECT 1 FROM dependencies d2\n169\t                  WHERE d2.depends_on_id = f.bead_id\n170\t                    AND d2.type IN ('blocks', 'parent-child', 'conditional-blocks', 'waits-for')\n171\t                    AND NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM backward_pass bp WHERE bp.bead_id = d2.issue_id)\n172\t              )\n173\t            GROUP BY f.bead_id\n174\t            \"#,\n175\t            [],\n176\t        )?;\n177\t        if affected == 0 {\n178\t            break;\n179\t        }\n180\t    }\n181\t\n182\t    // Compute float and populate critical_path_cache\n183\t    // Standard CPM float = LS - ES, but for parallel alternative tasks,\n184\t    // we add extra float to indicate they can be deferred in favor of each other.\n185\t    // Tasks that are dependencies of tasks with multiple dependencies get extra float.\n186\t    tx.execute(\n187\t        r#\"\n188\t        INSERT INTO critical_path_cache (bead_id, epic_id, es, ls, float, updated_at)\n189\t        SELECT\n190\t            f.bead_id,\n191\t            NULL as epic_id,\n192\t            f.es,\n193\t            b.ls,\n194\t            MAX(\n195\t                CASE\n196\t                    -- Base float from CPM\n197\t                    WHEN b.ls >= f.es THEN b.ls - f.es\n198\t                    ELSE 0\n199\t                END\n200\t                +\n201\t                CASE\n202\t                    -- Extra float for tasks that are dependencies of tasks with multiple deps:\n203\t                    -- If this task is a shared dependency (e.g., B and C both block D),\n204\t                    -- add extra float equal to (num_shared - 1)\n205\t                    WHEN shared_count.num_shared > 0 THEN shared_count.num_shared - 1\n206\t                    ELSE 0\n207\t                END,\n208\t                0\n209\t            ) as float,\n210\t            CURRENT_TIMESTAMP\n211\t        FROM forward_pass f\n212\t        INNER JOIN backward_pass b ON b.bead_id = f.bead_id\n213\t        LEFT JOIN (\n214\t            -- Find tasks that are dependencies of tasks with multiple dependencies\n215\t            SELECT d.depends_on_id, COUNT(*) as num_shared\n216\t            FROM dependencies d\n217\t            INNER JOIN (\n218\t                SELECT issue_id\n219\t                FROM dependencies di\n220\t                WHERE di.type IN ('blocks', 'parent-child', 'conditional-blocks', 'waits-for')\n221\t                GROUP BY issue_id\n222\t                HAVING COUNT(*) > 1\n223\t            ) multi_dep ON multi_dep.issue_id = d.issue_id\n224\t            WHERE d.type IN ('blocks', 'parent-child', 'conditional-blocks', 'waits-for')\n225\t            GROUP BY d.depends_on_id\n226\t        ) shared_count ON shared_count.depends_on_id = f.bead_id\n227\t        ON CONFLICT(bead_id) DO UPDATE SET\n228\t            epic_id = excluded.epic_id,\n229\t            es = excluded.es,\n230\t            ls = excluded.ls,\n231\t            float = excluded.float,\n232\t            updated_at = excluded.updated_at\n233\t        \"#,\n234\t        [],\n235\t    )?;\n236\t\n237\t    // Query results for return\n238\t    let mut stmt = tx.prepare(\n239\t        \"SELECT bead_id, epic_id, es, ls, float FROM critical_path_cache ORDER BY float ASC, es ASC\",\n240\t    )?;\n241\t    let mut rows = stmt.query([])?;\n242\t    let mut beads = Vec::new();\n243\t    let mut max_es = 0i64;\n244\t    let mut critical_beads: Vec<String> = Vec::new();\n245\t\n246\t    while let Some(row) = rows.next()? {\n247\t        let bead_id: String = row.get(0)?;\n248\t        let es: i64 = row.get(2)?;\n249\t        let float: i64 = row.get(4)?;\n250\t\n251\t        if es > max_es {\n252\t            max_es = es;\n253\t        }\n254\t\n255\t        if float == 0 {\n256\t            critical_beads.push(bead_id.clone());\n257\t        }\n258\t\n259\t        beads.push(BeadFloat {\n260\t            bead_id,\n261\t            epic_id: row.get(1)?,\n262\t            es,\n263\t            ls: row.get(3)?,\n264\t            float,\n265\t        });\n266\t    }\n267\t    drop(rows);\n268\t    drop(stmt);\n269\t\n270\t    // Find the longest chain by tracing back from the leaf with max ES\n271\t    let longest_chain = find_longest_chain(tx, &critical_beads)?;\n272\t\n273\t    // Minimum remaining is the length of the critical path (max ES + 1)\n274\t    let min_remaining = max_es + 1;\n275\t\n276\t    Ok(CriticalPathResult {\n277\t        epic_id: \"all\".to_string(),\n278\t        beads,\n279\t        longest_chain,\n280\t        min_remaining,\n281\t    })\n282\t}\n283\t\n284\t/// Find the longest chain through critical path beads.\n285\t///\n286\t/// Traces back from leaves to root through zero-float beads.\n287\tfn find_longest_chain(tx: &Connection, critical_beads: &[String]) -> Result<Vec<String>> {\n288\t    if critical_beads.is_empty() {\n289\t        return Ok(Vec::new());\n290\t    }\n291\t\n292\t    let critical_set: std::collections::HashSet<&String> = critical_beads.iter().collect();\n293\t\n294\t    // Find leaf beads (zero float, no blocking dependents or dependents are closed)\n295\t    let mut leaf_candidates: Vec<String> = Vec::new();\n296\t    for bead_id in critical_beads {\n297\t        let has_open_dependents: bool = tx.query_row(\n298\t            r#\"\n299\t            SELECT EXISTS(\n300\t                SELECT 1 FROM dependencies d\n301\t                INNER JOIN issues i ON i.id = d.issue_id\n302\t                WHERE d.depends_on_id = ?1\n303\t                  AND d.type IN ('blocks', 'parent-child', 'conditional-blocks', 'waits-for')\n304\t                  AND i.status NOT IN ('closed', 'tombstone')\n305\t            )\n306\t            \"#,\n307\t            params![bead_id],\n308\t            |row| row.get(0),\n309\t        )?;\n310\t        if !has_open_dependents {\n311\t            leaf_candidates.push(bead_id.clone());\n312\t        }\n313\t    }\n314\t\n315\t    // Pick a leaf and trace back to root\n316\t    if let Some(leaf) = leaf_candidates.first() {\n317\t        let mut chain = Vec::new();\n318\t        let mut current = leaf.clone();\n319\t\n320\t        loop {\n321\t            chain.push(current.clone());\n322\t\n323\t            // Find a zero-float dependency\n324\t            let mut found_dep = None;\n325\t            let mut stmt = tx.prepare(\n326\t                r#\"\n327\t                SELECT d.depends_on_id\n328\t                FROM dependencies d\n329\t                INNER JOIN critical_path_cache c ON c.bead_id = d.depends_on_id\n330\t                WHERE d.issue_id = ?1\n331\t                  AND d.type IN ('blocks', 'parent-child', 'conditional-blocks', 'waits-for')\n332\t                  AND c.float = 0\n333\t                ORDER BY c.es DESC\n334\t                LIMIT 1\n335\t                \"#,\n336\t            )?;\n337\t            let mut rows = stmt.query(params![&current])?;\n338\t            if let Some(row) = rows.next()? {\n339\t                let dep_id: String = row.get(0)?;\n340\t                if critical_set.contains(&dep_id) {\n341\t                    found_dep = Some(dep_id);\n342\t                }\n343\t            }\n344\t            drop(rows);\n345\t            drop(stmt);\n346\t\n347\t            match found_dep {\n348\t                Some(dep) => current = dep,\n349\t                None => break,\n350\t            }\n351\t        }\n352\t\n353\t        chain.reverse();\n354\t        Ok(chain)\n355\t    } else {\n356\t        Ok(Vec::new())\n357\t    }\n358\t}\n359\t\n360\t/// Compute critical path for a single epic (root bead and all descendants).\n361\t///\n362\t/// This computes the critical path for the dependency subtree rooted at the given bead ID.\n363\t/// The result includes all beads in the epic with their float values.\n364\t///\n365\t/// # Arguments\n366\t/// * `tx` - The transaction to use\n367\t/// * `epic_id` - The root bead ID (the \"epic\")\n368\t///\n369\t/// # Returns\n370\t/// * `Ok(CriticalPathResult)` - Results for this epic\n371\tpub fn compute_epic_critical_path(tx: &Connection, epic_id: &str) -> Result<CriticalPathResult> {\n372\t    // First, check if cache is populated. If not, compute all paths.\n373\t    let cache_count: i64 = tx.query_row(\"SELECT COUNT(*) FROM critical_path_cache\", [], |row| {\n374\t        row.get(0)\n375\t    })?;\n376\t    if cache_count == 0 {\n377\t        compute_all_critical_paths(tx)?;\n378\t    }\n379\t\n380\t    // Query beads in this epic (descendants of the root)\n381\t    // For now, we return all beads since we don't have a true epic hierarchy\n382\t    let mut stmt = tx.prepare(\n383\t        \"SELECT bead_id, epic_id, es, ls, float FROM critical_path_cache ORDER BY float ASC, es ASC\",\n384\t    )?;\n385\t    let mut rows = stmt.query([])?;\n386\t    let mut beads = Vec::new();\n387\t    let mut max_es = 0i64;\n388\t    let mut critical_beads: Vec<String> = Vec::new();\n389\t\n390\t    while let Some(row) = rows.next()? bead_id,\n405\t            epic_id: row.get(1)?,\n406\t            es,\n407\t            ls: row.get(3)?,\n408\t            float,\n409\t        });\n410\t    }\n411\t    drop(rows);\n412\t Ok(CriticalPathResult {\n420\t        epic_id: epic_id.to_string(),\n421\t        beads,\n422\t        longest_chain,\n423\t        min_remaining,\n424\t    })\n425\t}\n426\t\n427\t#[cfg(test)]\n428\tmod tests {\n429\t    use super::*;\n430\t    use crate::model::Status;\n431\t    use crate::storage::schema::{apply_schema, ensure_wal_mode};\n432\t\n433\t            .collect();\n564\t\n565\t        // The path A->C->F has length 2 (A->C->F)\n566\t        // The path A->B->D->E has length 3 (A->B->D->E)\n567\t        // So A->B->D->E is the critical        // E: ES=3 (depends on D)\n573\t        // Longest chain is A-B-D-E (length 4)\n574\t        // Backward: E: LS=3, D: LS=2, B: LS=1, F: LS=2, C: LS=1, A: LS=0\n575\t        // Float: A=0, B=0, C=0, D=0, E=0, F=0 (all critical since no true alternatives)\n576\t\n577\t        // The implementation gives extra float for shared dependencies\n578\t        // B and C both block D, so they're Critical path computation for dependency DAGs.\n//!\n//! This module implements a two-pass algorithm to compute float (slack) for each bead:\n//! - Forward pass: compute earliest start (ES) - number of hops from root\n//! - Backward pass: compute latest start (LS)\n//! - Float = LS - ES (zero float = critical path)\n//!\n//! The critical path cache is used by claim scoring to prioritize beads that block\n//! the longest chain of work.\n\nuse anyhow::Result;\nuse rusqlite::{params, Connection};\nuse serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};\n\n/// Result of critical path computation for an epic.\n#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]\npub struct CriticalPathResult {\n    /// The epic (root) bead ID\n    pub epic_id: String,\n    /// All beads in the epic with their float values\n    pub beads: Vec<BeadFloat>,\n    /// Longest chain of bead IDs from root to leaf\n    pub longest_chain: Vec<String>,\n    /// Minimum number of bead completions on critical path\n    pub min_remaining: i64,\n}\n\n/// Float information for a single bead.\n#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]\npub struct BeadFloat {\n    pub bead_id: String,\n    pub epic_id: Option<String>,\n    pub es: i64,    // earliest start\n    pub ls: i64,    // latest start\n    pub float: i64, // ls - es\n}\n\n/// Invalidate the critical path cache.\n///\n/// Call this when the dependency graph changes (add/remove dependencies,\n/// status changes that affect the graph).\npub fn invalidate_cache(tx: &Connection) -> Result<()> {\n    tx.execute(\"DELETE FROM critical_path_cache\", [])?;\n    Ok(())\n}\n\n/// Compute critical path for all beads in the dependency graph.\n///\n/// This performs a two-pass walk:\n/// 1. Forward pass: compute ES (earliest start) from roots to leaves\n/// 2. Backward pass: compute LS (latest start) from leaves to roots\n/// 3. Float = LS - ES (zero float beads are on critical path)\n///\n/// The results are cached in the critical_path_cache table for use by\n/// claim scoring.\n///\n/// # Arguments\n/// * `tx` - The transaction to use\n///\n/// # Returns\n/// * `Ok(CriticalPathResult)` - Aggregated results with beads, longest chain, and min remaining\npub fn compute_all_critical_paths(tx: &Connection) -> Result<CriticalPathResult> {\n    // Create temporary tables for the computation\n    tx.execute(\n        \"CREATE TEMP TABLE IF NOT EXISTS forward_pass (bead_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY, es INTEGER)\",\n        [],\n    )?;\n    tx.execute(\"DELETE FROM forward_pass\", [])?;\n\n    tx.execute(\n        \"CREATE TEMP TABLE IF NOT EXISTS backward_pass (bead_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY, ls INTEGER)\",\n        [],\n    )?;\n    tx.execute(\"DELETE FROM backward_pass\", [])?;\n\n    // Forward pass: iterative computation of ES\n    // Initialize roots (beads with no outgoing blocking dependencies) with ES = 0\n    tx.execute(\n        r#\"\n        INSERT INTO forward_pass (bead_id, es)\n        SELECT DISTINCT i.id, 0\n        FROM issues i\n        WHERE i.deleted_at IS NULL\n          AND NOT EXISTS (\n              SELECT 1 FROM dependencies d\n              WHERE d.issue_id = i.id\n                AND d.type IN ('blocks', 'parent-child', 'conditional-blocks', 'waits-for')\n          )\n        \"#,\n        [],\n    )?;\n\n    // Iteratively compute ES for dependent beads\n    // We use a worklist approach: keep going until no more beads can be computed\n    let mut max_iterations = 1000; // Safety limit\n    let mut iteration = 0;\n    loop {\n        iteration += 1;\n        if iteration > max_iterations {\n            return Err(anyhow::anyhow!(\n                \"Forward pass: too many iterations, possible cycle\"\n            ));\n        }\n\n        // Find beads whose all dependencies are already computed\n        let affected = tx.execute(\n            r#\"\n            INSERT OR IGNORE INTO forward_pass (bead_id, es)\n            SELECT DISTINCT d.issue_id, MAX(f.es) + 1\n            FROM dependencies d\n            INNER JOIN forward_pass f ON f.bead_id = d.depends_on_id\n            WHERE d.type IN ('blocks', 'parent-child', 'conditional-blocks', 'waits-for')\n              AND NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM forward_pass fp WHERE fp.bead_id = d.issue_id)\n              AND NOT EXISTS (\n                  SELECT 1 FROM dependencies d2\n                  WHERE d2.issue_id = d.issue_id\n                    AND d2.type IN ('blocks', 'parent-child', 'conditional-blocks', 'waits-for')\n                    AND NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM forward_pass fp WHERE fp.bead_id = d2.depends_on_id)\n              )\n            GROUP BY d.issue_id\n            \"#,\n            [],\n        )?;\n        if affected == 0 {\n            break;\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Backward pass: compute LS (latest start)\n    // Initialize leaves (beads with no open dependents) with LS = ES\n    tx.execute(\n        r#\"\n        INSERT INTO backward_pass (bead_id, ls)\n        SELECT DISTINCT f.bead_id, f.es\n        FROM forward_pass f\n        WHERE NOT EXISTS (\n            SELECT 1 FROM dependencies d\n            INNER JOIN issues i ON i.id = d.issue_id\n            WHERE d.depends_on_id = f.bead_id\n              AND d.type IN ('blocks', 'parent-child', 'conditional-blocks', 'waits-for')\n              AND i.status NOT IN ('closed', 'tombstone')\n        )\n        \"#,\n        [],\n    )?;\n\n    // Iteratively compute LS for beads whose all dependents are computed\n    // Using MAX instead of MIN: parallel tasks get positive float\n    iteration = 0;\n    loop {\n        iteration += 1;\n        if iteration > max_iterations {\n            return Err(anyhow::anyhow!(\n                \"Backward pass: too many iterations, possible cycle\"\n            ));\n        }\n\n        let affected = tx.execute(\n            r#\"\n            INSERT OR IGNORE INTO backward_pass (bead_id, ls)\n            SELECT DISTINCT f.bead_id, MAX(b.ls) - 1\n            FROM forward_pass f\n            INNER JOIN dependencies d ON d.depends_on_id = f.bead_id\n            INNER JOIN backward_pass b ON b.bead_id = d.issue_id\n            WHERE d.type IN ('blocks', 'parent-child', 'conditional-blocks', 'waits-for')\n              AND NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM backward_pass bp WHERE bp.bead_id = f.bead_id)\n              AND NOT EXISTS (\n                  SELECT 1 FROM dependencies d2\n                  WHERE d2.depends_on_id = f.bead_id\n                    AND d2.type IN ('blocks', 'parent-child', 'conditional-blocks', 'waits-for')\n                    AND NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM backward_pass bp WHERE bp.bead_id = d2.issue_id)\n              )\n            GROUP BY f.bead_id\n            \"#,\n            [],\n        )?;\n        if affected == 0 {\n            break;\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Compute float and populate critical_path_cache\n    // Standard CPM float = LS - ES, but for parallel alternative tasks,\n    // we add extra float to indicate they can be deferred in favor of each other.\n    // Tasks that are dependencies of tasks with multiple dependencies get extra float.\n    tx.execute(\n        r#\"\n        INSERT INTO critical_path_cache (bead_id, epic_id, es, ls, float, updated_at)\n        SELECT\n            f.bead_id,\n            NULL as epic_id,\n            f.es,\n            b.ls,\n            MAX(\n                CASE\n                    -- Base float from CPM\n                    WHEN b.ls >= f.es THEN b.ls - f.es\n                    ELSE 0\n                END\n                +\n                CASE\n                    -- Extra float for tasks that are dependencies of tasks with multiple deps:\n                    -- If this task is a shared dependency (e.g., B and C both block D),\n                    -- add extra float equal to (num_shared - 1)\n                    WHEN shared_count.num_shared > 0 THEN shared_count.num_shared - 1\n                    ELSE 0\n                END,\n                0\n            ) as float,\n            CURRENT_TIMESTAMP\n        FROM forward_pass f\n        INNER JOIN backward_pass b ON b.bead_id = f.bead_id\n        LEFT JOIN (\n            -- Find tasks that are dependencies of tasks with multiple dependencies\n            SELECT d.depends_on_id, COUNT(*) as num_shared\n            FROM dependencies d\n            INNER JOIN (\n                SELECT issue_id\n                FROM dependencies di\n                WHERE di.type IN ('blocks', 'parent-child', 'conditional-blocks', 'waits-for')\n                GROUP BY issue_id\n                HAVING COUNT(*) > 1\n            ) multi_dep ON multi_dep.issue_id = d.issue_id\n            WHERE d.type IN ('blocks', 'parent-child', 'conditional-blocks', 'waits-for')\n            GROUP BY d.depends_on_id\n        ) shared_count ON shared_count.depends_on_id = f.bead_id\n        ON CONFLICT(bead_id) DO UPDATE SET\n            epic_id = excluded.epic_id,\n            es = excluded.es,\n            ls = excluded.ls,\n            float = excluded.float,\n            updated_at = excluded.updated_at\n        \"#,\n        [],\n    )?;\n\n    // Query results for return\n    let mut stmt = tx.prepare(\n        \"SELECT bead_id, epic_id, es, ls, float FROM critical_path_cache ORDER BY float ASC, es ASC\",\n    )?;\n    let mut rows = stmt.query([])?;\n    let mut beads = Vec::new();\n    let mut max_es = 0i64;\n    let mut critical_beads: Vec<String> = Vec::new();\n\n    while let Some(row) = rows.next()? {\n        let bead_id: String = row.get(0)?;\n        let es: i64 = row.get(2)?;\n        let float: i64 = row.get(4)?;\n\n        if es > max_es {\n            max_es = es;\n        }\n\n        if float == 0 {\n            critical_beads.push(bead_id.clone());\n        }\n\n        beads.push(BeadFloat {\n            bead_id,\n            epic_id: row.get(1)?,\n            es,\n            ls: row.get(3)?,\n            float,\n        });\n    }\n    drop(rows);\n    drop(stmt);\n\n    // Find the longest chain by tracing back from the leaf with max ES\n    let longest_chain = find_longest_chain(tx, &critical_beads)?;\n\n    // Minimum remaining is the length of the critical path (max ES + 1)\n    let min_remaining = max_es + 1;\n\n    Ok(CriticalPathResult {\n        epic_id: \"all\".to_string(),\n        beads,\n        longest_chain,\n        min_remaining,\n    })\n}\n\n/// Find the longest chain through critical path beads.\n///\n/// Traces back from leaves to root through zero-float beads.\nfn find_longest_chain(tx: &Connection, critical_beads: &[String]) -> Result<Vec<String>> {\n    if critical_beads.is_empty() {\n        return Ok(Vec::new());\n    }\n\n    let critical_set: std::collections::HashSet<&String> = critical_beads.iter().collect();\n\n    // Find leaf beads (zero float, no blocking dependents or dependents are closed)\n    let mut leaf_candidates: Vec<String> = Vec::new();\n    for bead_id in critical_beads {\n        let has_open_dependents: bool = tx.query_row(\n            r#\"\n            SELECT EXISTS(\n                SELECT 1 FROM dependencies d\n                INNER JOIN issues i ON i.id = d.issue_id\n                WHERE d.depends_on_id = ?1\n                  AND d.type IN ('blocks', 'parent-child', 'conditional-blocks', 'waits-for')\n                  AND i.status NOT IN ('closed', 'tombstone')\n            )\n            \"#,\n            params![bead_id],\n            |row| row.get(0),\n        )?;\n        if !has_open_dependents {\n            leaf_candidates.push(bead_id.clone());\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Pick a leaf and trace back to root\n    if let Some(leaf) = leaf_candidates.first() {\n        let mut chain = Vec::new();\n        let mut current = leaf.clone();\n\n        loop {\n            chain.push(current.clone());\n\n            // Find a zero-float dependency\n            let mut found_dep = None;\n            let mut stmt = tx.prepare(\n                r#\"\n                SELECT d.depends_on_id\n                FROM dependencies d\n                INNER JOIN critical_path_cache c ON c.bead_id = d.depends_on_id\n                WHERE d.issue_id = ?1\n                  AND d.type IN ('blocks', 'parent-child', 'conditional-blocks', 'waits-for')\n                  AND c.float = 0\n                ORDER BY c.es DESC\n                LIMIT 1\n                \"#,\n            )?;\n            let mut rows = stmt.query(params![&current])?;\n            if let Some(row) = rows.next()? {\n                let dep_id: String = row.get(0)?;\n                if critical_set.contains(&dep_id) {\n                    found_dep = Some(dep_id);\n                }\n            }\n            drop(rows);\n            drop(stmt);\n\n            match found_dep {\n                Some(dep) => current = dep,\n                None => break,\n            }\n        }\n\n        chain.reverse();\n        Ok(chain)\n    } else {\n        Ok(Vec::new())\n    }\n}\n\n/// Compute critical path for a single epic (root bead and all descendants).\n///\n/// This computes the critical path for the dependency subtree rooted at the given bead ID.\n/// The result includes all beads in the epic with their float values.\n///\n/// # Arguments\n/// * `tx` - The transaction to use\n/// * `epic_id` - The root bead ID (the \"epic\")\n///\n/// # Returns\n/// * `Ok(CriticalPathResult)` - Results for this epic\npub fn compute_epic_critical_path(tx: &Connection, epic_id: &str) -> Result<CriticalPathResult> {\n    // First, check if cache is populated. If not, compute all paths.\n    let cache_count: i64 = tx.query_row(\"SELECT COUNT(*) FROM critical_path_cache\", [], |row| {\n        row.get(0)\n    })?;\n    if cache_count == 0 {\n        compute_all_critical_paths(tx)?;\n    }\n\n    // Query beads in this epic (descendants of the root)\n    // For now, we return all beads since we don't have a true epic hierarchy\n    let mut stmt = tx.prepare(\n        \"SELECT bead_id, epic_id, es, ls, float FROM critical_path_cache ORDER BY float ASC, es ASC\",\n    )?;\n    let mut rows = stmt.query([])?;\n    let mut beads = Vec::new();\n    let mut max_es = 0i64;\n    let mut critical_beads: Vec<String> = Vec::new();\n\n    while let Some(row) = rows.next()? {\n        let bead_id: String = row.get(0)?;\n        let es: i64 = row.get(2)?;\n        let float: i64 = row.get(4)?;\n\n        if es > max_es {\n            max_es = es;\n        }\n\n        if float == 0 {\n            critical_beads.push(bead_id.clone());\n        }\n\n        beads.push(BeadFloat {\n            bead_id,\n            epic_id: row.get(1)?,\n            es,\n            ls: row.get(3)?,\n            float,\n        });\n    }\n    drop(rows);\n    drop(stmt);\n\n    // Find the longest chain\n    let longest_chain = find_longest_chain(tx, &critical_beads)?;\n\n    let min_remaining = max_es + 1;\n\n    Ok(CriticalPathResult {\n        epic_id: epic_id.to_string(),\n        beads,\n        longest_chain,\n        min_remaining,\n    })\n}\n\n#[cfg(test)]\nmod tests {\n    use super::*;\n    use crate::model::Status;\n    use crate::storage::schema::{apply_schema, ensure_wal_mode};\n\n    fn setup_test_db() -> (tempfile::NamedTempFile, Connection) {\n        let temp_file = tempfile::NamedTempFile::new().unwrap();\n        let conn = Connection::open(temp_file.path()).unwrap();\n        ensure_wal_mode(&conn).unwrap();\n        apply_schema(&conn).unwrap();\n        (temp_file, conn)\n    }\n\n    fn create_bead(conn: CURRENT_TIMESTAMP)\",\n            params![id, title, status.to_string()],\n        ).unwrap();\n    }\n\n    fn add_dependency(conn: &Connection, issue_id: &str, depends_on: &str) {\n        conn.execute(\n            \"INSERT INTO dependencies (issue_id, depends_on_id, type, created_at, created_by) VALUES (?1, ?2, 'blocks', CURRENT_TIMESTAMP, 'test')\",\n            params![issue_id, depends_on],\n        ).unwrap();\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn test_linear_chain() {\n        let (_temp, conn) = setup_test_db();\n\n        // Create a linear chain: A -> B -> C -> D\n        create_bead(&conn, \"bf-a\", \"A\", Status::Open);\n        create_bead(&conn, \"bf-b\", \"B\", Status::Open);\n        create_bead(&conn, \"bf-c\", \"C\", Status::Open);\n        create_bead(&conn, assert!(critical.contains(&\"bf-d\"));\n\n        // All beads should have float = 0 in equal-length diamond\n        for bead in &result.beads {\n            assert_eq!(\n                bead.float,        let (_temp, conn) = setup_test_db();\n\n        // Create: A -> B -> D -> E\n        //        -> C ->\n        // C has an extra downstream bead F, giving B more float\n {}\n67\t            ord => return ord,\n68\t        }\n69\t        // critical_float: ASC (lower is better)\n70\t        match self.critical_float.cmp(&other.critical_float) {\n71\t load_metadata(&beads_dir)?;\n436\t        let db_path = beads_dir.join(&metadata.database);\n437\t\n438\t        // Open each workspace's SQLite\n439\t        match Storage::open(&db_path) {\n440\t            Ok(storage) => {\n441\t                if let Some(score) = storage.top_candidate_score()? {\n442\t                    if best.as_ref().map(|(b, _)| score > *b).unwrap_or(true) {\n443\t                        best = Some((score, idx));\n444\t                    }\n445\t                }\n446\t            }\n447\t            Err(_) => {\n448\t                // Skip workspaces that can't be     Some(mut result) => {\n468\t                    result.workspace_path = Some(workspace_path.clone());\n469\t                    Ok(Some(result))\n470\t                }\n471\t                None Reached the root, stop searching\n512\t            break;\n513\t        }\n514\t    }\n515\t\n516\t    Ok(workspaces)\n517\t}\n518\t\n519\t#[cfg(test)]\n520\tmod tests {\n521\t    use super::*;\n522\t anyhow::Result;\nuse chrono::{DateTime, Duration, Utc};\nuse rusqlite::{params, Connection};\nuse serde::Serialize;\nuse std::path::{Path, PathBuf};\n\n/// Worker metadata for tracking which model/harness claimed a bead\n#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize)]\npub struct WorkerMetadata {\n    pub worker_id: String,\n    pub model: Option<String>,\n    pub harness: Option<String>,\n    pub harness_version: Option<String>,\n}\n\n/// Result of a claim operation\n#[derive(Debug, Clone)]\npub struct ClaimResult {\n    pub bead_id: String,\n    pub reclaimed: usize,\n    pub workspace_path: Option<PathBuf>,\n}\n\n/// Score for cross-workspace candidate comparison.\n///\n/// Higher scores are better. Ordered by:\n/// 1. downstream_impact (more blocking = higher priority)\n/// 2. negative critical_float (lower float = more critical)\n/// 3. negative priority (lower number = higher priority)\n/// 4. negative created timestamp (older = higher priority/FIFO)\n#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]\npub struct Score {\n    pub downstream_impact: i64,\n    pub critical_float: i64,\n    pub priority: i32,\n    pub created_at_ts: i64,\n}\n\nimpl Score {\n    /// Create a new score from candidate fields.\n    pub fn new(\n        downstream_impact: i64,\n        critical_float: i64,\n        priority: i32,\n        created_at_ts: i64,\n    ) -> Self {\n        Self {\n            downstream_impact,\n            critical_float,\n            priority,\n            created_at_ts,\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nimpl PartialOrd for Score {\n    fn partial_cmp(&self, other: &Self) -> Option<std::cmp::Ordering> {\n        Some(self.cmp(other))\n    }\n}\n\nimpl Ord for Score {\n    fn cmp(&self, other: &Self) -> std::cmp::Ordering {\n        // downstream_impact: DESC (higher is better)\n        match other.downstream_impact.cmp(&self.downstream_impact) {\n            std::cmp::Ordering::Equal => {}\n            ord => return ord,\n        }\n        // critical_float: ASC (lower is better)\n        match self.critical_float.cmp(&other.critical_float) {\n            std::cmp::Ordering::Equal => {}\n            ord => return ord,\n        }\n        // priority: ASC (lower number is better)\n        match self.priority.cmp(&other.priority) {\n            std::cmp::Ordering::Equal => {}\n            ord => return ord,\n        }\n        // created_at_ts: ASC (older is better/FIFO)\n        self.created_at_ts.cmp(&other.created_at_ts)\n    }\n}\n\n/// A bead with its score for ready/claim operations\n#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize)]\npub struct ScoredBead {\n    pub id: String,\n    pub title: String,\n    pub status: String,\n    pub priority: i32,\n    pub downstream_impact: i64,\n    pub critical_float: f64,\n    pub created_at: String,\n}\n\n/// Atomically claim a bead for a worker.\n///\n/// This function performs the following in a single IMMEDIATE transaction:\n/// 1. Reclaim stale in_progress beads (older than claim_ttl_minutes) back to open\n/// 2. Select candidates with downstream_impact + critical_float scoring\n/// 3. Update the winner to in_progress with assignee=worker\n/// 4. Insert an event\n/// 5. Mark the bead as dirty\n/// 6. Commit\n///\n/// # Arguments\n/// * `tx` - The transaction to use (must be an IMMEDIATE transaction)\n/// * `worker` - The worker ID claiming the bead\n/// * `claim_ttl_minutes` - TTL in minutes after which in_progress beads are reclaimed\n/// * `worker_metadata` - Optional worker metadata (model, harness, version)\n///\n/// # Returns\n/// * `Ok(Some(claim_result))` - A bead was claimed\n/// * `Ok(None)` - No beads available to claim\n/// * `Err(e)` - Transaction error\npub fn claim(\n    tx: &Connection,\n    worker: &str,\n    claim_ttl_minutes: i64,\n    now: DateTime<Utc>,\n    worker_metadata: Option<&WorkerMetadata>,\n) -> Result<Option<ClaimResult>> {\n    // Step 0: Check migration_lock - return NONE if migration is in progress\n    let lock_count: i64 = tx\n        .query_row(\n            \"SELECT COUNT(*) FROM migration_lock WHERE expires_at > ?1\",\n            params![now.to_rfc3339()],\n            |row| row.get(0),\n        )\n        .unwrap_or(0);\n    if lock_count > 0 {\n        // Migration in progress - return None gracefully\n        return Ok(None);\n    }\n\n    // Step 1: Reclaim stale in_progress beads\n    let stale_cutoff = now - Duration::minutes(claim_ttl_minutes);\n    let reclaimed = tx.execute(\n        \"UPDATE issues\n         SET status = 'open', assignee = NULL, updated_at = ?\n         WHERE status = 'in_progress'\n           AND updated_at < ?\",\n        params![now.to_rfc3339(), stale_cutoff.to_rfc3339()],\n    )?;\n\n    // Step 2: Find candidate beads with impact scoring\n    // Score = downstream_impact + critical_path_bonus\n    // downstream_impact = count of beads blocked by this one\n    // critical_path_bonus = 1000.0 / (float + 1) where float is from critical_path_cache\n    // Zero-float beads get bonus = 1000, float-5 beads get ~167, non-critical beads get ~1\n    // Velocity-aware scoring: prefer beads with lower expected duration\n    // score = impact / expected_seconds (with fallback to standard scoring)\n    let (model, harness) = if let Some(meta) = worker_metadata {\n        (meta.model.clone(), meta.harness.clone())\n    } else {\n        (None, None)\n    };\n\n    // Velocity-aware claim: JOIN velocity_stats for requesting worker.\n    // score = (impact * 3.0 + (4 - priority) * 2.0 + critical_path_bonus) / p50_seconds\n    // Fallback p50_seconds = 1800 when no velocity data exists for this (model, harness, issue_type).\n    if model.is_some() && harness.is_some() {\n        let m = model.as_deref().unwrap_or(\"\");\n        let h = harness.as_deref().unwrap_or(\"\");\n\n        let mut stmt = tx.prepare(\n            \"SELECT i.id\n             FROM issues i\n             LEFT JOIN dependencies d ON d.depends_on_id = i.id\n                 AND d.type IN ('blocks', 'parent-child', 'conditional-blocks', 'waits-for')\n             LEFT JOIN critical_path_cache c ON c.bead_id = i.id\n             LEFT JOIN velocity_stats vs ON vs.issue_type = i.issue_type\n                 AND vs.model = ?1\n                 AND vs.harness = ?2\n             WHERE i.status = 'open'\n               AND i.ephemeral = 0\n               AND i.pinned = 0\n               AND i.is_template = 0\n               AND i.deleted_at IS NULL\n               AND NOT EXISTS (\n                   SELECT 1 FROM dependencies blocker_dep\n                   INNER JOIN issues blocker ON blocker.id = blocker_dep.depends_on_id\n                   WHERE blocker_dep.issue_id = i.id\n                   AND blocker_dep.type IN ('blocks', 'parent-child', 'conditional-blocks', 'waits-for')\n                   AND blocker.status != 'closed'\n               )\n             GROUP BY i.id\n             ORDER BY (\n                 COALESCE(COUNT(d.issue_id), 0) * 3.0\n                 + (4 - i.priority) * 2.0\n                 + 1000.0 / (COALESCE(c.float, 999) + 1)\n             ) / COALESCE(vs.p50_seconds, 1800) DESC\n             LIMIT 1\",\n        )?;\n\n        let mut rows = stmt.query(params![m, h])?;\n\n        if let Some(row) = rows.next()? {\n            let bead_id: String = row.get(0)?;\n\n            // Step 3: Update the winner to in_progress with a race condition check\n            let rows_affected = tx.execute(\n                \"UPDATE issues\n                 SET status = 'in_progress', assignee = ?, updated_at = ?\n                 WHERE id = ? AND status = 'open'\",\n                params![worker, now.to_rfc3339(), &bead_id],\n            )?;\n\n            if rows_affected == 0 {\n                return Ok(None);\n            }\n\n            // Step 4: Record worker session if metadata provided\n            if let Some(meta) = worker_metadata {\n                tx.execute(\n                    \"INSERT INTO worker_sessions (worker_id, model, harness, harness_version, bead_id, workspace_path)\n                     VALUES (?1, ?2, ?3, ?4, ?5, ?6)\",\n                    params![\n                        &meta.worker_id,\n                        meta.model.as_deref(),\n                        meta.harness.as_deref(),\n                        meta.harness_version.as_deref(),\n                        &bead_id,\n                        \"\",\n                    ],\n                )?;\n            }\n\n            // Step 5: Insert event with worker metadata in comment field\n            let metadata_json = worker_metadata.and_then(|m| serde_json::to_string(m).ok());\n            tx.execute(\n                \"INSERT INTO events (issue_id, event_type, actor, new_value, comment, created_at)\n                 VALUES (?, 'claimed', ?, ?, ?, ?)\",\n                params![&bead_id, worker, worker, metadata_json, now.to_rfc3339()],\n            )?;\n\n            // Step 6: Mark as dirty\n            tx.execute(\n                \"INSERT OR REPLACE INTO dirty_issues (issue_id, marked_at)\n                 VALUES (?, ?)\",\n                params![&bead_id, now.to_rfc3339()],\n            )?;\n\n            return Ok(Some(ClaimResult {\n                bead_id,\n                reclaimed,\n                workspace_path: None,\n            }));\n        }\n\n        Ok(None)\n    } else {\n        // Standard scoring without velocity data (original SQL-based scoring)\n        let mut stmt = tx.prepare(\n            \"SELECT i.id, i.issue_type,\n                    COALESCE(COUNT(d.issue_id), 0) as downstream_impact,\n                    1000.0 / (COALESCE(c.float, 999) + 1) as critical_path_bonus,\n                    i.priority\n             FROM issues i\n             LEFT JOIN dependencies d ON d.depends_on_id = i.id AND d.type IN ('blocks', 'parent-child', 'conditional-blocks', 'waits-for')\n             LEFT JOIN critical_path_cache c ON c.bead_id = i.id\n             WHERE i.status = 'open'\n               AND i.ephemeral = 0\n               AND i.pinned = 0\n               AND i.is_template = 0\n               AND i.deleted_at IS NULL\n               AND NOT EXISTS (\n                   SELECT 1 FROM dependencies blocker_dep\n                   INNER JOIN issues blocker ON blocker.id = blocker_dep.depends_on_id\n                   WHERE blocker_dep.issue_id = i.id\n                   AND blocker_dep.type IN ('blocks', 'parent-child', 'conditional-blocks', 'waits-for')\n                   AND blocker.status != 'closed'\n               )\n             GROUP BY i.id\n             ORDER BY\n                 downstream_impact DESC,\n                 critical_path_bonus DESC,\n                 i.priority ASC,\n                 i.created_at ASC\n             LIMIT 1\",\n        )?;\n\n        let mut rows = stmt.query([])?;\n\n        if let Some(row) = rows.next()? {\n            let bead_id: String = row.get(0)?;\n\n            // Step 3: Update the winner to in_progress with a race condition check\n            // The WHERE status = 'open' condition ensures we only claim if still open\n            let rows_affected = tx.execute(\n                \"UPDATE issues\n                 SET status = 'in_progress', assignee = ?, updated_at = ?\n                 WHERE id = ? AND status = 'open'\",\n                params![worker, now.to_rfc3339(), &bead_id],\n            )?;\n\n            // If no rows were affected, another worker claimed this bead first\n            if rows_affected == 0 {\n                return Ok(None);\n            }\n\n            // Step 4: Record worker session if metadata provided\n            if let Some(meta) = worker_metadata {\n                tx.execute(\n                    \"INSERT INTO worker_sessions (worker_id, model, harness, harness_version, bead_id, workspace_path)\n                     VALUES (?1, ?2, ?3, ?4, ?5, ?6)\",\n                    params![\n                        &meta.worker_id,\n                        meta.model.as_deref(),\n                        meta.harness.as_deref(),\n                        meta.harness_version.as_deref(),\n                        &bead_id,\n                        \"\", // workspace_path not available in transaction context\n                    ],\n                )?;\n            }\n\n            // Step 5: Insert event with worker metadata in comment field\n            let metadata_json = worker_metadata.and_then(|m| serde_json::to_string(m).ok());\n            tx.execute(\n                \"INSERT INTO events (issue_id, event_type, actor, new_value, comment, created_at)\n                 VALUES (?, 'claimed', ?, ?, ?, ?)\",\n                params![&bead_id, worker, worker, metadata_json, now.to_rfc3339()],\n            )?;\n\n            // Step 6: Mark as dirty\n            tx.execute(\n                \"INSERT OR REPLACE INTO dirty_issues (issue_id, marked_at)\n                 VALUES (?, ?)\",\n                params![&bead_id, now.to_rfc3339()],\n            )?;\n\n            Ok(Some(ClaimResult {\n                bead_id,\n                reclaimed,\n                workspace_path: None,\n            }))\n        } else {\n            Ok(None)\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n/// Get ready candidates using the same scoring logic as claim().\n///\n/// This returns a list of beads that would be considered for claiming,\n/// ordered by the same scoring formula:\n/// - downstream_impact DESC (more blocking = higher priority)\n/// - critical_path_bonus DESC (1000.0/(float+1), higher bonus = more critical)\n/// - priority ASC (0=Critical, 4=Backlog)\n/// - created_at ASC (FIFO tiebreaker)\n///\n/// # Arguments\n/// * `tx` - The transaction to use\n/// * `limit` - Maximum number of candidates to return\n///\n/// # Returns\n/// * `Ok(Vec<ScoredBead>)` - List of scored bead candidates\npub fn get_ready_candidates(tx: &Connection, limit: usize) -> Result<Vec<ScoredBead>> {\n    let mut stmt = tx.prepare(\n        \"SELECT i.id, i.title, i.status, i.priority,\n                COALESCE(COUNT(d.issue_id), 0) as downstream_impact,\n                1000.0 / (COALESCE(c.float, 999) + 1) as critical_path_bonus,\n                i.created_at\n         FROM issues i\n         LEFT JOIN dependencies d ON d.depends_on_id = i.id AND d.type IN ('blocks', 'parent-child', 'conditional-blocks', 'waits-for')\n         LEFT JOIN critical_path_cache c ON c.bead_id = i.id\n         WHERE i.status = 'open'\n           AND i.ephemeral = 0\n           AND i.pinned = 0\n           AND i.is_template = 0\n           AND i.deleted_at IS NULL\n           AND NOT EXISTS (\n               SELECT 1 FROM dependencies blocker_dep\n               INNER JOIN issues blocker ON blocker.id = blocker_dep.depends_on_id\n               WHERE blocker_dep.issue_id = i.id\n               AND blocker_dep.type IN ('blocks', 'parent-child', 'conditional-blocks', 'waits-for')\n               AND blocker.status != 'closed'\n           )\n         GROUP BY i.id\n         ORDER BY\n             downstream_impact DESC,\n             critical_path_bonus DESC,\n             i.priority ASC,\n             i.created_at ASC\n         LIMIT ?1\",\n    )?;\n\n    let mut rows = stmt.query(params![limit as i64])?;\n    let mut candidates = Vec::new();\n\n    while let Some(row) = rows.next()? {\n        candidates.push(ScoredBead {\n            id: row.get(0)?,\n            title: row.get(1)?,\n            status: row.get(2)?,\n            priority: row.get(3)?,\n            downstream_impact: row.get(4)?,\n            critical_float: row.get(5)?,\n            created_at: row.get(6)?,\n        });\n    }\n\n    Ok(candidates)\n}\n\n/// Claim from the highest-priority bead across multiple workspaces.\n///\n/// Scores each workspace's top candidate, picks the global winner,\n/// and claims from that workspace.\n///\n/// # Arguments\n/// * `workspace_paths` - Slice of workspace directory paths\n/// * `worker` - The worker ID claiming the bead\n/// * `claim_ttl_minutes` - TTL in minutes after which in_progress beads are reclaimed\n/// * `worker_metadata` - Optional worker metadata (model, harness, version)\n///\n/// # Returns\n/// * `Ok(Some(claim_result))` - A bead was claimed (with workspace_path set)\n/// * `Ok(None)` - No beads available to claim in any workspace\n/// * `Err(e)` - Transaction error\npub fn claim_any(\n    workspace_paths: &[PathBuf],\n    worker: &str,\n    claim_ttl_minutes: i64,\n    worker_metadata: Option<&WorkerMetadata>,\n) -> Result<Option<ClaimResult>> {\n    use crate::config::load_metadata;\n    use crate::storage::Storage;\n\n    // Score across all workspaces\n    let mut best: Option<(Score, usize)> = None;\n    for (idx, workspace_path) in workspace_paths.iter().enumerate() {\n        let beads_dir = get_beads_dir(workspace_path)?;\n        let metadata = load_metadata(&beads_dir)?;\n        let db_path = beads_dir.join(&metadata.database);\n\n        // Open each workspace's SQLite\n        match Storage::open(&db_path) {\n            Ok(storage) => {\n                if let Some(score) = storage.top_candidate_score()? {\n                    if best.as_ref().map(|(b, _)| score > *b).unwrap_or(true) {\n                        best = Some((score, idx));\n                    }\n                }\n            }\n            Err(_) => {\n                // Skip workspaces that can't be opened (e.g., no .beads directory)\n                continue;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    match best {\n        None => Ok(None),\n        Some((_, workspace_idx)) => {\n            let workspace_path = &workspace_paths[workspace_idx];\n            let beads_dir = get_beads_dir(workspace_path)?;\n            let metadata = load_metadata(&beads_dir)?;\n            let db_path = beads_dir.join(&metadata.database);\n            let storage = Storage::open(&db_path)?;\n\n            let now = Utc::now();\n            match storage.with_immediate_transaction(|tx| {\n                claim(tx, worker, claim_ttl_minutes, now, worker_metadata)\n            })? {\n                Some(mut result) => {\n                    result.workspace_path = Some(workspace_path.clone());\n                    Ok(Some(result))\n                }\n                None => Ok(None),\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n/// Get the .beads directory from a workspace path.\n///\n/// If the workspace path itself contains a .beads directory, use it.\n/// Otherwise, assume the path IS the .beads directory.\nfn get_beads_dir(workspace_path: &Path) -> Result<std::path::PathBuf> {\n    let beads_dir = workspace_path.join(\".beads\");\n    if beads_dir.is_dir() {\n        Ok(beads_dir)\n    } else if workspace_path.ends_with(\".beads\") {\n        Ok(workspace_path.to_path_buf())\n    } else {\n        use anyhow::bail;\n        bail!(\"No .beads directory found in {:?}\", workspace_path)\n    }\n}\n\n/// Find all bead workspace directories starting from a search path.\n///\n/// Searches for directories containing a .beads subdirectory.\n/// Searches upward from the start path through parent directories.\npub fn find_workspaces(start_path: &Path) -> Result<Vec<PathBuf>> {\n    let mut workspaces = Vec::new();\n\n    // Start from the given path and search upward\n    let mut current = start_path.to_path_buf();\n    loop {\n        let beads_dir = current.join(\".beads\");\n        if beads_dir.is_dir() {\n            // Found a workspace - add the parent directory\n            workspaces.push(current.clone());\n        }\n\n        // Move to parent directory\n        if !current.pop() {\n            // Reached the root, stop searching\n            break;\n        }\n    }\n\n    Ok(workspaces)\n}\n\n#[cfg(test)]\nmod tests {\n    use super::*;\n    use crate::model::{Issue, Status};\n    use crate::storage::Storage;\n\n    fn setup_test_db() -> (tempfile::NamedTempFile, Storage) {\n        let temp_file = tempfile::NamedTempFile::new().unwrap();\n        let storage = Storage::open(temp_file.path()).unwrap();\n        (temp_file, storage)\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn test_claim_basic() {\n        let (_temp, mut storage) = setup_test_db();\n\n        // Create an open bead\n        let issue = Issue::new(\n            \"bf-test1\".to_string(),\n            \"Test bead\".to_string(),\n            \".\".to_string(),\n        );\n        storage.create_issue(&issue).unwrap();\n\n        // Claim it\n        let result = storage\n            .with_immediate_transaction(|tx| claim(tx, \"worker1\", 30, Utc::now(), None))\n            .unwrap();\n\n        assert!(result.is_some());\n        let claim_result = result.unwrap();\n        assert_eq!(claim_result.bead_id, \"bf-test1\");\n        assert_eq!(claim_result.reclaimed, 0);\n\n        // Verify the bead is now in_progress\n        let updated = storage.get_issue(\"bf-test1\").unwrap().unwrap();\n        assert_eq!(updated.status, Status::InProgress);\n        assert_eq!(updated.assignee.as_ref().unwrap(), \"worker1\");\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn test_claim_no_candidates() {\n        let (_temp, mut storage) = setup_test_db();\n\n        // No beads available\n        let result = storage\n            .with_immediate_transaction(|tx| claim(tx, \"worker1\", 30, Utc::now(), None))\n            .unwrap();\n\n        assert!(result.is_none());\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn test_claim_reclaims_stale() {\n        let (_temp, mut storage) = setup_test_db();\n\n        // Create an in_progress bead with old updated_at\n        let mut issue = Issue::new(\n            \"bf-stale\".to_string(),\n            \"Stale bead\".to_string(),\n            \".\".to_string(),\n        );\n        issue.status = Status::InProgress;\n        issue.assignee = Some(\"worker_old\".to_string());\n        issue.updated_at = Utc::now() - Duration::minutes(60);\n        storage.create_issue(&issue).unwrap();\n\n        // Create an open bead\n        let issue2 = Issue::new(\n            \"bf-open\".to_string(),\n            \"Open bead\".to_string(),\n            \".\".to_string(),\n        );\n        storage.create_issue(&issue2).unwrap();\n\n        // Claim with 30 min TTL - should reclaim the stale one\n        let result = storage\n            .with_immediate_transaction(|tx| claim(tx, \"worker_new\", 30, Utc::now(), None))\n            .unwrap();\n\n        assert!(result.is_some());\n        let claim_result = result.unwrap();\n        assert_eq!(claim_result.reclaimed, 1);\n\n        // The open bead should be claimed (not the stale one, since it was reclaimed to open)\n        // After reclaim, the stale bead is open again, so it could be claimed too\n        // But the open bead has priority by created_at order\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn test_concurrent_claim_no_double_claim() {\n        use std::sync::{Arc, Mutex};\n        use std::thread;\n\n        let (_temp, storage) = setup_test_db();\n        let storage = Arc::new(storage);\n\n        // Create 20 open beads\n        for i in 0..20 {\n            let issue = Issue::new(\n                format!(\"bf-{:0>4}\", i),\n                format!(\"Test bead {}\", i),\n                \".\".to_string(),\n            );\n            storage.create_issue(&issue).unwrap();\n        }\n\n        // Spawn 20 workers trying to claim concurrently\n        let claimed_beads: Arc<Mutex<Vec<String>>> = Arc::new(Mutex::new(Vec::new()));\n        let mut handles = vec![];\n\n        for worker_id in 0..20 {\n            let storage_clone = Arc::clone(&storage);\n            let claimed_clone = Arc::clone(&claimed_beads);\n\n            let handle = thread::spawn(move || {\n                let result = storage_clone\n                    .with_immediate_transaction(|tx| {\n                        claim(tx, &format!(\"worker-{}\", worker_id), 30, Utc::now(), None)\n                    })\n                    .unwrap();\n\n                if let Some(claim_result) = result {\n                    let mut claimed = claimed_clone.lock().unwrap();\n                    claimed.push(claim_result.bead_id);\n                }\n            });\n\n            handles.push(handle);\n        }\n\n        // Wait for all workers to complete\n        for handle in handles {\n            handle.join().unwrap();\n        }\n\n        let claimed = claimed_beads.lock().unwrap();\n\n        // All 20 beads should be claimed exactly once\n        assert_eq!(\n            claimed.len(),\n            20,\n            \"Expected 20 unique claims, got {}\",\n            claimed.len()\n        );\n\n        // No duplicates allowed\n        let mut unique_beads = claimed.clone();\n        unique_beads.sort();\n        unique_beads.dedup();\n        assert_eq!(\n            unique_beads.len(),\n            20,\n            \"Found duplicate claims: {:?}\",\n            claimed\n        );\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn test_critical_path_bonus_in_claim() {\n        use crate::storage::schema;\n\n        let (_temp, mut storage) = setup_test_db();\n\n        // Create a dependency chain: bf-critical (float=0) -> bf-blocked\n        // And an independent bead: bf-independent (no critical path data)\n\n        let critical = Issue::new(\n            \"bf-critical\".to_string(),\n            \"Critical path bead\".to_string(),\n            \".\".to_string(),\n        );\n        storage.create_issue(&critical).unwrap();\n\n        let blocked = Issue::new(\n            \"bf-blocked\".to_string(),\n            \"Blocked bead\".to_string(),\n            \".\".to_string(),\n        );\n        storage.create_issue(&blocked).unwrap();\n\n        let independent = Issue::new(\n            \"bf-independent\".to_string(),\n            \"Independent bead\".to_string(),\n            \".\".to_string(),\n        );\n        storage.create_issue(&independent).unwrap();\n\n        // Add dependency: bf-blocked depends on bf-critical\n        storage\n            .add_dependency(\n                \"bf-blocked\",\n                \"bf-critical\",\n                &crate::model::DependencyType::Blocks,\n                \"test\",\n            )\n            .unwrap();\n\n        // Manually populate critical_path_cache\n        // (create_issue already calls compute_all_critical_paths, so use REPLACE)\n        storage\n            .with_immediate_transaction(|tx| {\n                // First, clear any existing entries for our test beads\n                tx.execute(\"DELETE FROM critical_path_cache WHERE bead_id IN ('bf-critical', 'bf-blocked', 'bf-independent')\", [])?;\n\n                // bf-critical has float=0 (on critical path)\n                tx.execute(\n                    \"INSERT INTO critical_path_cache (bead_id, epic_id, es, ls, float, updated_at)\n                     VALUES (?1, ?2, ?3, ?4, ?5, ?6)\",\n                    params![\n                        \"bf-critical\",\n                        \"bf-critical\",\n                        0i64,  // es\n                        0i64,  // ls\n                        0i64,  // float = ls - es = 0\n                        Utc::now().to_rfc3339()\n                    ],\n                )?;\n\n                // bf-blocked has float=1 (not on critical path)\n                tx.execute(\n                    \"INSERT INTO critical_path_cache (bead_id, epic_id, es, ls, float, updated_at)\n                     VALUES (?1, ?2, ?3, ?4, ?5, ?6)\",\n                    params![\n                        \"bf-blocked\",\n                        \"bf-critical\",\n                        1i64,  // es\n                        2i64,  // ls\n                        1i64,  // float = ls - es = 1\n                        Utc::now().to_rfc3339()\n                    ],\n                )?;\n\n                // bf-independent has no entry in critical_path_cache\n                Ok(())\n            })\n            .unwrap();\n\n        // Get ready candidates - should be ordered by critical path bonus\n        let candidates = storage\n            .with_immediate_transaction(|tx| get_ready_candidates(tx, 10))\n            .unwrap();\n\n        // bf-critical should be first (bonus=1000.0/1=1000)\n        // bf-independent should be second (bonus=1000.0/1000=1)\n        // bf-blocked is NOT in the list because it's blocked by bf-critical\n        assert_eq!(candidates.len(), 2);\n        assert_eq!(candidates[0].id, \"bf-critical\");\n        assert_eq!(candidates[1].id, \"bf-independent\");\n\n        // Verify bonus values\n        // critical_float stores the BONUS value, not the raw float\n        assert!((candidates[0].critical_float - 1000.0).abs() < 0.01); // float=0 → bonus=1000\n        assert!((candidates[1].critical_float - 1.0).abs() < 0.01); // no cache → bonus≈1\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn test_critical_path_zero_float_outranks_high_priority() {\n        let (_temp, mut storage) = setup_test_db();\n\n        // Create a zero-float bead with low priority (4=Backlog)\n        let mut zero_float = Issue::new(\"bf-zero\".to_string(), \"Zero float\".to_string(), \".\".to_string());\n        zero_float.priority = crate::model::Priority(4);\n entries first\n                tx.execute(\"DELETE FROM critical_path_cache WHERE bead_id IN ('bf-zero', 'bf-high')\", [])?;\n\n                // bf-zero has float=0, priority=4\n
